🟩 Healthy Poke nació como un concepto fresco y disruptivo dentro de la restauración rápida saludable en España. **Post**: https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andreas-donner-peakcare_peakcareai-hospitalityai-hoteloperations-activity-7465289784454991874-1jIZ?utm_source=combined_share_message&utm_medium=member_desktop&rcm=ACoAAC_zUH4BqBNtDG2Tido_zWQLNa9WQ1N85kU

Hotel teams often solve daily pressure before management has a formal AI strategy.  A reservation employee drafts guest replies with AI. A sales manager uses AI to prepare offer text. Front office teams test translation tools. Guest inquiries are summarised, copied, rephrased or pre-qualified with tools nobody has officially approved.  This is where Shadow AI starts.  The problem is not that hotel teams use AI.  The problem is that nobody may know: • which guest data is being entered • which tools are being used • which workflows are affected • who is responsible • where operational risk begin...
